    SELECT [t1].[Id], [t1].[DateAssignation], [t1].[DateCreation], [t1].[DateFinIntervention], [t1].[DateIntervention], [t1].[DateSuppression], [t1].[EstSupprime], [t1].[MontantDevis], [t1].[MontantFacture], [t1].[Observations], [t1].[PrestataireId], [t1].[ReferenceDevis], [t1].[ReferenceFacture], [t1].[TicketIncidentId], [p0].[Id] AS [Id0], [p0].[Actif], [p0].[Adresse], [p0].[Courriel], [p0].[DateCreation] AS [DateCreation0], [p0].[DateSuppression] AS [DateSuppression0], [p0].[EstSupprime] AS [EstSupprime0], [p0].[Nom], [p0].[NomEntreprise], [p0].[NumeroContribuable], [p0].[Observations] AS [Observations0], [p0].[Prenom], [p0].[SocieteId], [p0].[Specialite], [p0].[Telephone], [p0].[TypePrestataire], [p0].[Ville]
    FROM [TicketsIntervenants] AS [t1]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[Actif], [p].[Adresse], [p].[Courriel], [p].[DateCreation], [p].[DateSuppression], [p].[EstSupprime], [p].[Nom], [p].[NomEntreprise], [p].[NumeroContribuable], [p].[Observations], [p].[Prenom], [p].[SocieteId], [p].[Specialite], [p].[Telephone], [p].[TypePrestataire], [p].[Ville]
        FROM [Prestataires] AS [p]
        WHERE [p].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [p0] ON [t1].[PrestataireId] = [p0].[Id]
    WHERE [t1].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [s0] ON [s].[Id] = [s0].[TicketIncidentId]
ORDER BY [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [s].[Id1], [s].[Id2], [s].[Id3], [s].[Id4], [t2].[Id], [s0].[Id]
